# Donor NAME-GAP census, ranked by demand (kit_laws --donor-deps --census; §824 reshaped by §828)

Donor project-API symbols with no same-name receiver definition. A NAME
MISS IS NOT A FINDING (tale §828): each row carries mechanical evidence
for §828's rules — twin: = renamed candidate (compare signatures);
family: = same-API-family symbols (check for the capability under a
different architecture); no-candidate = highest suspicion, still a
question. Demand rank = triage priority, and the planner demand axis.
